[codex] ## Implementation Guardrails
[codex] 
[codex] ### Architecture and API boundaries
[codex] 
[codex] - Keep feature code under `src/features/`; features must not import from other
[codex]   features. Put shared code in the appropriate top-level `src/` directory.
[codex] - Use the `@/*` alias instead of relative source imports.
[codex] - Components use TanStack Query hooks and mutation callbacks; they do not call
[codex]   Axios directly or copy server data into Redux.
[codex] - Match generated OpenAPI constraints in forms and keep
[codex]   `src/utils/errorMessages.ts` synchronized with backend application codes.
[codex] - Access the local application through
[codex]   `https://app.budgetanalyzer.localhost`, not the Vite port.
[codex] 
[codex] ### React and components
[codex] 
[codex] Read the [hooks guide](docs/react-hooks-lifecycle-mental-model.md) before
[codex] changing hooks or lifecycle behavior.
[codex] 
[codex] - Use effects only to synchronize external systems such as DOM APIs, timers,
[codex]   listeners, subscriptions, and imperative libraries. Clean up installed or
[codex]   acquired resources.
[codex] - Derive values during render, run user-driven work in event handlers, and use
[codex]   TanStack Query for server state.
[codex] - Keep components synchronous. Use `mutate(data, { onSuccess, onError })`
[codex]   instead of `mutateAsync` with component-level `async`/`await`.
[codex] - Memoize functions passed as props with `useCallback`.
[codex] - Do not put IIFEs, multi-line logic, or inline function definitions in JSX
[codex]   props; extract named callbacks or helpers.
[codex] - Never disable an ESLint rule without permission.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Authorization
[codex] 
[codex] - Roles choose layout chrome; never use `isAdmin` to gate an action.
[codex] - Permissions gate actions and feature access. Use `PermissionGuard` for a
[codex]   route or subtree and `usePermission` for an individual affordance.
[codex] - Denied `PermissionGuard` children must remain unmounted so their queries do
[codex]   not run.
[codex] - Use unscoped transaction permissions for a user's own data and `:any` only
[codex]   for distinct cross-user features.
[codex] - Call `usePermission` only at hook-safe top-level sites, never inside a filter,
[codex]   loop, or callback.
[codex] 
[codex] The [authentication owner](docs/authentication.md#roles-and-permissions)
[codex] contains the complete taxonomy and gating guidance.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Centralized conventions
[codex] 
[codex] - Define reusable animations in `src/lib/animations.ts`.
[codex] - Import date behavior only through `src/utils/dates.ts`, never directly from
[codex]   `date-fns`. LocalDate values have no timezone; ISO 8601 values do.
[codex] - Add dynamic table width mappings to `src/utils/columnWidth.ts`; never use a
[codex]   React `style` prop.
[codex] - Do not add transient toasts or a toast dependency. Keep actionable feedback
[codex]   persistent and contextual; informative cross-route results may use the
[codex]   existing flash-banner pattern. Follow the contract in
[codex]   [API integration](docs/api-integration.md#user-facing-error-messages).
[codex] - Shared `src/components/ui/Table.tsx` owns horizontal overflow. Feature tables
[codex]   must not hide its native scrollbar.
[codex] - Do not add tooltips; information must remain available on mobile.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Strict CSP
[codex] 
[codex] Application code must comply with `style-src 'self'` without `unsafe-inline` or
[codex] `unsafe-eval` and with the repository's stricter Tailwind-first conventions.
[codex] 
[codex] - Never add React `style={...}` props.
[codex] - Never add application code or dependencies that create runtime `&lt;style>`
[codex]   elements, insert CSSOM rules, write stylesheet content, or generate CSS with
[codex]   `eval()` or `new Function()`.
[codex] - Use `acquireBodyScrollLock()` from `src/utils/bodyScrollLock.ts` for overlay
[codex]   scroll locks.
[codex] - Treat bundle matches as capabilities to investigate, not as an allowlist or
[codex]   proof of execution. A serialized DOM `style` attribute alone is not proof of
[codex]   a browser CSP violation.
[codex] - Before adding a UI dependency, changing Motion usage or versions, or changing
[codex]   overlays, follow the static-scan and browser-audit requirements in
[codex]   [Architecture](docs/architecture.md#content-security-policy),
[codex]   [Development](docs/development.md#production-smoke-build-and-dropdown-gate),
[codex]   and [Testing](docs/testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness).
[codex] 
[codex] ### Testing
[codex] 
[codex] - Put production-code tests beside their owner in `__tests__/`; reserve
[codex]   `src/testing/` for shared infrastructure.
[codex] - Add a meaningful test for new behavior or explain explicitly why none is
[codex]   needed.
[codex] - Use MSW for new API-facing behavior unless a direct module mock is
[codex]   intentionally narrower.
[codex] - Prefer `@testing-library/user-event` for workflows and `fireEvent` only for
[codex]   low-level synthetic events.
[codex] - Test product behavior, not native browser, React, TypeScript, or third-party
[codex]   library behavior.
[codex] - Changes to `playwright.config.ts` or `e2e/` require
[codex]   `npm run typecheck:e2e`. Browser tests require the user-managed environment;
[codex]   agents must not start Tilt or Vite.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Working Rules
[codex] 
[codex] - Never run `npm run dev`; the user controls the development server.
[codex] - Use `npm run lint:fix`, not `npm run lint`, for agent validation.
[codex] - Never perform git write operations such as commit, push, checkout, or reset
[codex]   without an explicit user request. The user controls git operations.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Before finishing, run the gates that apply to the changed surface:
[codex] 
[codex] - Documentation-only changes: verify changed links and referenced paths, then
[codex]   run `git diff --check`. Application tests are not required when runtime,
[codex]   build, and test behavior are unchanged.
[codex] - Application, test, and E2E source: format changed files with the repository's
[codex]   Prettier configuration. Run `npm run format` only when its scope contains no
[codex]   unrelated user work.
[codex] - Production application changes: run `npm run lint:fix`, focused Vitest tests
[codex]   while iterating, and `npm run build` before handoff. The build includes the
[codex]   full coverage, TypeScript, and bundle gates.
[codex] - Test-only changes: run `npm run lint:fix` and the affected tests. Run
[codex]   `npm run test:coverage` when changing shared test infrastructure, coverage
[codex]   configuration, or behavior exercised across features.
[codex] - Changes to `playwright.config.ts` or `e2e/`: run `npm run lint:fix` and
[codex]   `npm run typecheck:e2e`. Run browser tests only when the user-managed
[codex]   environment is available and the Testing guide requires them.
[codex] - UI dependency, Motion, overlay, or CSP-sensitive changes: also run the static
[codex]   scan and browser-audit gates required by the Strict CSP section.
[codex] 
[codex] If a required verifier cannot run because a tool, credential, service, browser,
[codex] or user-managed environment is unavailable, report that explicitly. Do not
[codex] claim the work is fully verified.
[codex]

[codex]   [orchestration setup](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/development/getting-started.md)
[codex] 
[codex] This repository uses `package-lock.json`; use npm so dependency resolution stays
[codex] consistent with CI and the container builds.

[codex] ## Installation and Local Runtime
[codex] 
[codex] Install the frontend dependencies and create a local environment file:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] npm install
[codex] cp .env.example .env
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] The supported full-stack runtime is owned by the sibling orchestration
[codex] repository. Its host-managed `./setup.sh` and `tilt up` flow creates the local
[codex] cluster, TLS, ingress, backend services, and the Vite workload. Do not start
[codex] Tilt, Vite, or another development server on a user's behalf.
[codex] 
[codex] Once the workloads are healthy, open:
[codex] 
[codex] ```text
[codex] https://app.budgetanalyzer.localhost
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Do not browse directly to `http://localhost:3000`. Authentication, protected API
[codex] routing, TLS, and same-origin behavior depend on the Istio ingress route. The
[codex] sibling [local-environment guide](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/development/local-environment.md)
[codex] owns platform startup, local CA trust, Tilt, and ingress troubleshooting.
[codex] 
[codex] The development `Dockerfile` runs Vite as non-root UID/GID `1001` on port
[codex] `3000`. Tilt relies on `npm run dev -- --host 0.0.0.0 --port 3000` and syncs
[codex] frontend changes into that workload for HMR.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Repository Commands
[codex] 
[codex] The scripts in `package.json` are:
[codex] 
[codex] | Command | Purpose |
[codex] | --- | --- |
[codex] | `npm run dev` | Start Vite on port `3000`; user-managed local runtime only |
[codex] | `npm run preview` | Preview the most recent Vite build locally |
[codex] | `npm run build` | Run the coverage gate, TypeScript check, and standard production bundle |
[codex] | `npm run build:bundle` | Run the TypeScript check and standard production bundle without coverage |
[codex] | `npm run build:prod-smoke` | Build at `/_prod-smoke/` and run the temporary dropdown CSP gate |
[codex] | `npm run check:csp:dropdown` | Scan an existing `dist/`, source imports, and package metadata for known dropdown blockers |
[codex] | `npm run lint` | Check ESLint with zero warnings; used by CI |
[codex] | `npm run lint:fix` | Apply ESLint fixes, then fail on remaining errors or warnings; required agent workflow |
[codex] | `npm run format` | Format application, E2E, and Playwright source covered by the script |
[codex] | `npm test` | Run Vitest in watch mode |
[codex] | `npm run test:coverage` | Run Vitest once with V8 coverage and enforce thresholds |
[codex] | `npm run test:ui` | Start the Vitest UI |
[codex] | `npm run typecheck:e2e` | Type-check Playwright configuration and E2E sources |
[codex] | `npm run test:e2e:harness` | Run Playwright environment, fail-closed fixture, and CSP-detector self-tests |
[codex] | `npm run test:e2e:csp` | Run detector self-tests and strict application CSP workflows |
[codex] 
[codex] Use `npm run lint:fix` for agent changes; do not run `npm run lint` first. Changes
[codex] to `playwright.config.ts` or `e2e/` require an explicit
[codex] `npm run typecheck:e2e`. Focused and full-suite commands, browser prerequisites,
[codex] and artifacts are documented in the [Testing Guide](testing-guide.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## Environment Variables
[codex] 
[codex] Vite reads variables from `.env` and its standard mode-specific files. The
[codex] checked-in `.env.example` documents the supported frontend variables:
[codex] 
[codex] | Variable | Default | Purpose |
[codex] | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] | `VITE_API_BASE_URL` | `/api` | Same-origin base path used by the Axios client |
[codex] | `VITE_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS` | `120000` | Interval between active-session heartbeat calls |
[codex] | `VITE_WARNING_BEFORE_EXPIRY_SECONDS` | `120` | Lead time for the inactivity-expiry warning |
[codex] 
[codex] Keep `VITE_API_BASE_URL=/api` for the local ingress topology. Auth routes use
[codex] root-relative session-gateway paths and are not derived from this variable.
[codex] Authentication behavior is documented in
[codex] [Authentication and Authorization](authentication.md); transport behavior is
[codex] documented in [API Integration](api-integration.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## Project Structure
[codex] 
[codex] Feature pages, components, hooks, and utilities live under `src/features/`.
[codex] Shared infrastructure lives in top-level directories such as `src/api/`,
[codex] `src/components/`, `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/`, `src/store/`, `src/types/`, and
[codex] `src/utils/`; shared test support lives in `src/testing/`. Pages remain inside
[codex] their owning features rather than a separate top-level page directory.
[codex] 
[codex] [Architecture](architecture.md#application-structure) owns the detailed source
[codex] boundaries and feature organization.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Build & Deployment
[codex] 
[codex] All Vite build variants replace `dist/` with their output.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Standard Build
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] npm run build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] `build` runs `npm run test:coverage` and then `npm run build:bundle`.
[codex] `build:bundle` runs `tsc` followed by `vite build` and produces assets served
[codex] from `/`. The coverage gate requires `80%` statements, `80%` branches, `75%`
[codex] functions, and `80%` lines; the [Testing Guide](testing-guide.md#coverage) owns
[codex] coverage interpretation and exclusions.
[codex] 
[codex] The build workflow in `.github/workflows/build.yml` installs with `npm ci`,
[codex] lints, runs coverage, builds and checks the production-smoke variant, then
[codex] rebuilds the standard `/` bundle for the uploaded `dist/` artifact.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Production-Smoke Build and Dropdown Gate
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] npm run build:prod-smoke
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] This variant runs `tsc`, builds with the base path `/_prod-smoke/`, and then
[codex] runs `scripts/check-dropdown-csp.mjs`. Auth and API requests remain
[codex] root-relative. The sibling Tilt stack serves the resulting bundle at
[codex] `https://app.budgetanalyzer.localhost/_prod-smoke/` while the normal `/` route
[codex] continues to serve the Vite development application.
[codex] 
[codex] The dropdown check scans emitted text assets for known stylesheet-injection,
[codex] Radix menu, and `react-remove-scroll` signatures. It also rejects the former
[codex] Radix dropdown dependency in production source imports, `package.json`, and
[codex] `package-lock.json`. The standalone `npm run check:csp:dropdown` command expects
[codex] `dist/` to exist and fails closed if it cannot scan it.
[codex] 
[codex] This is an active, temporary dropdown-specific gate, not a general CSP proof.
[codex] It remains until equivalent desktop and mobile dropdown browser coverage exists.
[codex] The [Architecture CSP section](architecture.md#content-security-policy) owns the
[codex] policy meaning and removal condition; the
[codex] [external browser harness](testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness) owns
[codex] runtime evidence and current coverage limits.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Production Container
[codex] 
[codex] `Dockerfile.production` is the release image definition:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. A Node 20 build stage installs with `npm ci` and runs the full
[codex]    coverage-gated `npm run build`.
[codex] 2. An unprivileged NGINX image serves the static bundle on port `3000` using
[codex]    `nginx.production.conf`.
[codex] 
[codex] The Node stage is pinned to BuildKit's native build platform. The release
[codex] workflow can therefore build the requested `linux/arm64` runtime image without
[codex] running Vitest through target-architecture emulation. NGINX provides the SPA
[codex] fallback, a `/health` endpoint, immutable caching for `/assets/`, and no-cache
[codex] headers for application routes.
[codex] 
[codex] `.github/workflows/publish-release.yml` publishes the GHCR image for strict
[codex] `vX.Y.Z` tags or an explicit manual source ref and Docker label. It does not
[codex] publish `latest` and prints the digest-pinned image reference. Deployment and
[codex] release-inventory procedures are owned by the sibling
[codex] [orchestration CI/CD guide](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/ci-cd.md).
[codex] v24.20.0
[codex] 11.19.0

[codex] # Budget Analyzer Web
[codex] 
[codex] [![Build](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/budget-analyzer-web/actions/workflows/build.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/budget-analyzer-web/actions/workflows/build.yml)
[codex] 
[codex] React 19 single-page application for managing and analyzing financial
[codex] transactions. It provides the browser interface to the Budget Analyzer backend
[codex] and uses same-origin, server-side sessions.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Features
[codex] 
[codex] - Transaction search, sorting, pagination, editing, deletion, and statement import
[codex] - Saved transaction views and multi-currency analytics
[codex] - Fine-grained user and administrator workflows
[codex] - Statement-format and currency management
[codex] - Responsive light and dark interfaces
[codex] 
[codex] ## Quick Start
[codex] 
[codex] Install dependencies and create the local environment file:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] npm install
[codex] cp .env.example .env
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] The supported application runtime is provided by the sibling
[codex] [orchestration repository](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/development/getting-started.md).
[codex] After that environment is healthy, open
[codex] `https://app.budgetanalyzer.localhost`; do not use the Vite port directly.
[codex] 
[codex] See the [development guide](docs/development.md) for prerequisites, commands,
[codex] environment variables, and build variants.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Technology
[codex] 
[codex] React, TypeScript, Vite, React Router, TanStack Query, Redux Toolkit, TanStack
[codex] Table, Tailwind CSS, Axios, Vitest, Testing Library, MSW, and Playwright.
[codex]

[codex] name: Publish Release
[codex] 
[codex] on:
[codex]   push:
[codex]     tags:
[codex]       - 'v*'
[codex]   workflow_dispatch:
[codex]     inputs:
[codex]       release_ref:
[codex]         description: 'Existing Git ref to build; SemVer tags publish X.Y.Z by default'
[codex]         required: true
[codex]         type: string
[codex]       docker_label:
[codex]         description: 'Optional Docker tag label for an ad hoc manual image; never use latest'
[codex]         required: false
[codex]         type: string
[codex] 
[codex] permissions:
[codex]   contents: read
[codex] 
[codex] env:
[codex]   FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] 
[codex] jobs:
[codex]   publish:
[codex]     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex]     permissions:
[codex]       contents: read
[codex]       packages: write
[codex]     env:
[codex]       IMAGE_NAME: ghcr.io/budgetanalyzer/budget-analyzer-web
[codex] 
[codex]     steps:
[codex]       - name: Checkout code
[codex]         uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex]         with:
[codex]           ref: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && github.event.inputs.release_ref || github.ref_name }}
[codex]           fetch-depth: 0
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Resolve release inputs
[codex]         id: release
[codex]         shell: bash
[codex]         env:
[codex]           REQUESTED_RELEASE_REF: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && github.event.inputs.release_ref || github.ref_name }}
[codex]           REQUESTED_DOCKER_LABEL: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && github.event.inputs.docker_label || '' }}
[codex]         run: |
[codex]           set -euo pipefail
[codex] 
[codex]           event_name="${GITHUB_EVENT_NAME}"
[codex]           release_ref="${REQUESTED_RELEASE_REF}"
[codex]           requested_docker_label="${REQUESTED_DOCKER_LABEL}"
[codex] 
[codex]           if [[ -z "${release_ref}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "release_ref is required." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           if [[ "${release_ref}" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
[codex]             default_docker_label="${release_ref#v}"
[codex]           else
[codex]             default_docker_label=""
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           if [[ "${event_name}" == "push" ]]; then
[codex]             if [[ -z "${default_docker_label}" ]]; then
[codex]               echo "Release tag pushes must use SemVer tags like v1.2.3, got ${release_ref}." >&2
[codex]               exit 1
[codex]             fi
[codex]             if [[ -n "${requested_docker_label}" ]]; then
[codex]               echo "docker_label is only supported for workflow_dispatch." >&2
[codex]               exit 1
[codex]             fi
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           if [[ "${event_name}" == "workflow_dispatch" && -z "${default_docker_label}" && -z "${requested_docker_label}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "workflow_dispatch release_ref is not a SemVer release tag, so docker_label is required." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           docker_label="${requested_docker_label:-${default_docker_label}}"
[codex]           if [[ ! "${docker_label}" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$ || "${docker_label}" == "latest" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "Resolved docker_label is not Docker-safe or is disallowed: ${docker_label}." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           echo "docker_label=${docker_label}" >> "${GITHUB_OUTPUT}"
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Resolve checked-out revision
[codex]         id: source
[codex]         shell: bash
[codex]         run: |
[codex]           set -euo pipefail
[codex] 
[codex]           commit_sha="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
[codex]           echo "commit_sha=${commit_sha}" >> "${GITHUB_OUTPUT}"
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Set up QEMU
[codex]         uses: docker/setup-qemu-action@v4
[codex]         with:
[codex]           platforms: arm64
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Set up Docker Buildx
[codex]         uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v4
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Log in to GHCR
[codex]         uses: docker/login-action@v4
[codex]         with:
[codex]           registry: ghcr.io
[codex]           username: ${{ github.actor }}
[codex]           password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Build and push release image
[codex]         id: build
[codex]         uses: docker/build-push-action@v7
[codex]         with:
[codex]           context: .
[codex]           file: ./Dockerfile.production
[codex]           platforms: linux/arm64
[codex]           push: true
[codex]           tags: |
[codex]             ${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}:${{ steps.release.outputs.docker_label }}
[codex]           labels: |
[codex]             org.opencontainers.image.source=${{ github.server_url }}/${{ github.repository }}
[codex]             org.opencontainers.image.revision=${{ steps.source.outputs.commit_sha }}
[codex]             org.opencontainers.image.version=${{ steps.release.outputs.docker_label }}
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Print digest-pinned reference
[codex]         shell: bash
[codex]         run: |
[codex]           echo "${IMAGE_NAME}:${{ steps.release.outputs.docker_label }}@${{ steps.build.outputs.digest }}"

[codex] ## Phase 8: Onboard the frontend and audit its lockfile
[codex] 
[codex] ### Workspace
[codex] 
[codex] ../budget-analyzer-web
[codex] 
[codex] ### Goal
[codex] 
[codex] Discover direct/lockfile updates and preserve full versus production audit evidence.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Scope
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate npm/Dockerfile/Actions configuration, audit CI and nearest docs.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Non-goals
[codex] 
[codex] No package bumps, audit fixes, lockfile refresh during onboarding, or frontend code.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Required context
[codex] 
[codex] Read local instructions, package manifests/lockfile, Dockerfiles and existing CI,
[codex] plus the shared preset and frontend section of the saved review.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Execution steps
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Add the preset consumer; enable scheduled lockfile-maintenance PRs and scoped
[codex]    React/React DOM and Vitest grouping. Keep toolchain majors visible for approval.
[codex] 2. Reuse CI dependency installation and add weekly/manual full `npm audit` and
[codex]    `npm audit --omit=dev` reports. Preserve JSON output and distinguish audit
[codex]    findings from registry/installation errors.
[codex] 3. Verify Node build-image and workflow-version discovery alongside npm packages.
[codex]    Document report access and existing lint/test/CSP/build checks for bot PRs.
[codex]

[codex] 18:stack, External Secrets Operator, Kyverno, the frontend dependency tree,
[codex] 19-`go-redis`, the JDK runtime image, and selected observability components.

[codex] 80-FORM/DIGEST authentication, WebDAV, AJP, or direct HTTP/2 behavior that the
[codex] 81-current services do not appear to use. The affected components are still in
[codex] 82-the deployed classpath, so patching remains preferable to relying only on
[codex] 83:configuration non-use. Netty is directly relevant to the reactive Session
[codex] 84-Gateway and other WebFlux/WebClient paths.

[codex] 165-  The supported container path is Linux, but the patch is still low risk.

[codex] 167-  The application uses `BrowserRouter`, not those server modes. The open
[codex] 168-  redirect advisory is the most relevant browser-side reason to update.

[codex] 170:  `react-server-dom-*` package is installed. React 19.2.8 remains the sensible

[codex] [codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] [codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] [codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] [codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] [codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] [codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] [codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] [codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] [codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] [codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] [codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] [codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] [codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] [codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] [codex] +as a clean security result. A build step resolves and verifies the application
[codex] [codex] +and test trees before the official action performs its unfiltered graph pass;
[codex] [codex] +this prevents the graph task's zero exit status from masking unresolved
[codex] [codex] +configurations.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] [codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] [codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] [codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] [codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] [codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The credential-free Phase 7 run generated an official local plugin snapshot,
[codex] [codex] +but it is deliberately recorded as incomplete. It contained 87 build/tool
[codex] [codex] +coordinates and zero `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring WebFlux, Spring Security,
[codex] [codex] +Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, or Lettuce coordinates. Its only Spring runtime
[codex] [codex] +names were `spring-core` and `spring-jcl`, alongside build-plugin Jackson
[codex] [codex] +coordinates. Gradle's `runtimeClasspath` report marked every application

[codex] [codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] [codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] [codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] [codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] [codex] +require approval.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +```bash
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] [codex] +```
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] [codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] [codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] [codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] [codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] [codex] +

[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Audit
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '17 6 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +concurrency:
[codex] +  group: dependency-audit
[codex] +  cancel-in-progress: false
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  npm-audit:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up Node.js
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          node-version: '22'
[codex] +          cache: 'npm'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Prepare report directory
[codex] +        run: mkdir -p dependency-audit-reports
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install locked dependencies
[codex] +        run: npm ci
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Capture full and production audits
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -u
[codex] +
[codex] +          set +e
[codex] +          npm audit --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.stderr.log
[codex] +          full_status=$?
[codex] +          npm audit --omit=dev --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.stderr.log
[codex] +          production_status=$?
[codex] +          set -e
[codex] +
[codex] +          audit_operational_failure=0
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit() {
[codex] +            scope="$1"
[codex] +            report="$2"
[codex] +            command_status="$3"
[codex] +
[codex] +            if [[ ! -s "${report}" ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced no JSON report." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e 'type == "object"' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced invalid JSON." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if jq -e '.error != null' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              jq -r '"npm audit error: \(.error.code // "unknown"): \(.error.summary // .error.message // "no message")"' "${report}" >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total | numbers' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit JSON has no vulnerability metadata." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            total="$(jq -r '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total' "${report}")"
[codex] +            if [[ "${command_status}" -eq 0 && "${total}" -ne 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -eq 1 && "${total}" -eq 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -ne 0 && "${command_status}" -ne 1 ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit returned unexpected status ${command_status} for ${total} findings." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            jq -r --arg scope "${scope}" '
[codex] +              .metadata.vulnerabilities |
[codex] +              "| \($scope) | \(.critical) | \(.high) | \(.moderate) | \(.low) | \(.total) |"
[codex] +            ' "${report}" >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          }
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            echo '## npm audit results'
[codex] +            echo
[codex] +            echo '| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total |'
[codex] +            echo '| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |'
[codex] +          } >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Full dependency tree" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            "${full_status}"
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Production dependencies" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            "${production_status}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            printf 'full_status=%s\n' "${full_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'production_status=%s\n' "${production_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'operational_failure=%s\n' "${audit_operational_failure}"
[codex] +          } > dependency-audit-reports/status.txt
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${audit_operational_failure}" -ne 0 ]]; then
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${full_status}" -eq 1 || "${production_status}" -eq 1 ]]; then
[codex] +            echo 'Vulnerability findings were retained as evidence and do not fail this scheduled report.' >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload audit reports
[codex] +        if: ${{ always() }}
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: npm-audit-reports
[codex] +          path: dependency-audit-reports/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: warn
[codex] +          retention-days: 7

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,116 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration and audit workflow prepared; automation is not
[codex] +installed or active. Authenticated acceptance is pending the system-wide
[codex] +activation phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +This repository uses Renovate as the sole owner of dependency-update pull
[codex] +requests. GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts provide alert-only
[codex] +vulnerability evidence; Dependabot version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +requests must remain disabled. The shared operating policy is owned by the
[codex] +[orchestration dependency-automation guide](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the Budget Analyzer shared preset. Renovate discovers:
[codex] +
[codex] +- direct npm dependencies and lockfile selections from `package.json` and
[codex] +  `package-lock.json`;
[codex] +- Node and NGINX build/runtime images, tags, and digests in all three
[codex] +  Dockerfiles;
[codex] +- pinned GitHub Actions and `actions/setup-node` runtime inputs in the workflow
[codex] +  files.
[codex] +
[codex] +Routine updates and lockfile maintenance run on the shared weekly schedule.
[codex] +React and React DOM updates are grouped, as are the Vitest runner, UI, and
[codex] +coverage packages. The shared policy disables automerge, keeps patch, minor,
[codex] +and major proposals distinguishable, and requires Dependency Dashboard approval
[codex] +for majors. Toolchain majors such as TypeScript, Vite, Vitest, ESLint, Tailwind,
[codex] +React Router, and Node therefore remain visible for compatibility review rather
[codex] +than being excluded or constrained to historical target versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Lockfile maintenance is discovery work, not permission to run `npm audit fix` or
[codex] +silently refresh the checked-in lockfile during onboarding. Review dependency
[codex] +and lockfile diffs together. For image updates, retain immutable digests and
[codex] +verify the selected tag/digest supports the required platform before merging.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Audit evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` runs weekly and on manual dispatch. It
[codex] +installs exactly `package-lock.json` with `npm ci`, then independently captures:
[codex] +
[codex] +- `npm audit --json` as `npm-audit-full.json`;
[codex] +- `npm audit --omit=dev --json` as `npm-audit-production.json`.
[codex] +
[codex] +Download the `npm-audit-reports` artifact from the workflow run to compare the
[codex] +raw JSON reports. The artifact is retained for seven days and also includes each
[codex] +command's stderr and `status.txt`. The job summary presents severity counts for
[codex] +the two scopes.
[codex] +
[codex] +An npm audit exit status caused by findings does not fail this scheduled report;
[codex] +the existing backlog remains visible without blocking unrelated pull requests.
[codex] +Missing or malformed JSON, an npm-reported registry error, an unexpected command
[codex] +status, or an `npm ci` failure does fail the workflow. Installation errors remain
[codex] +separate in the named install step's log, while audit errors and partial report
[codex] +files remain available through the always-upload artifact step.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free onboarding run on 2026-09-06 reproduced the saved review
[codex] +against the unchanged lockfile:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total | Top-level vulnerable paths |
[codex] +| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] +| Full dependency tree | 3 | 13 | 2 | 2 | 20 | `@babel/core`, `@humanfs/node`, `@vitest/coverage-v8`, `@vitest/ui`, `axios`, `brace-expansion`, `browserslist`, `fflate`, `flatted`, `form-data`, `js-yaml`, `nanoid`, `picomatch`, `postcss`, `postcss-selector-parser`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom`, `vite`, `vitest`, `ws` |
[codex] +| Production dependencies | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 4 | `axios`, `form-data`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom` |
[codex] +
[codex] +Both commands returned status 1 because findings exist, produced valid
[codex] +vulnerability metadata, and reported no registry error. Compare advisory and
[codex] +package identities in future runs rather than relying on the historical total:
[codex] +new advisories can appear while old ones are remediated.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachability and runtime applicability remain human triage. In particular,
[codex] +development-server findings are not production-browser findings, and this SPA
[codex] +does not use React Server Components or React Router's server/framework modes.
[codex] +Those distinctions inform priority but do not justify dropping findings from the
[codex] +full report.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. The existing `Build`
[codex] +pull-request workflow runs locked installation, ESLint, the Vitest coverage gate,
[codex] +the production-smoke CSP gate, and the standard TypeScript/Vite bundle. Reviewers
[codex] +must also inspect release notes, the resolved lockfile change, browser and SSR/RSC
[codex] +applicability, strict-CSP behavior, and Node/image platform compatibility.
[codex] +
[codex] +The [development guide](development.md#repository-commands) owns local build and
[codex] +validation commands. CSP-sensitive dependency changes must also follow the
[codex] +[architecture CSP contract](architecture.md#content-security-policy) and the
[codex] +[external browser harness](testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness); the bot
[codex] +cannot infer runtime stylesheet injection or mobile/browser compatibility from
[codex] +a successful package lookup.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +No GitHub, registry, or Mend credentials are required or used during local
[codex] +preparation. The activation operator must complete and retain these checks:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration preset, install the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, and retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving the preset resolves and npm, lockfile-maintenance,
[codex] +   Dockerfile, GitHub Actions, and setup-node inputs are discovered without
[codex] +   authentication, configuration, timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. Dispatch or observe `.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` on trusted
[codex] +   `main`. Retain the successful workflow URL and `npm-audit-reports` artifact
[codex] +   proving `npm ci` succeeded and both raw JSON scopes were captured. A findings
[codex] +   exit status is expected; installation, registry, and malformed-report errors
[codex] +   must still fail the job.
[codex] +3. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   Dependabot update pull requests remain disabled. Retain alert URLs and map
[codex] +   advisory/package identities to the two audit reports, including explained
[codex] +   misses and production-versus-development applicability.
[codex] +4. Retain the successful existing `Build` workflow URL for representative bot
[codex] +   pull requests, including lockfile maintenance and a toolchain-major proposal
[codex] +   after dashboard approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns final cross-repository acceptance. Local
[codex] +preparation does not activate Renovate, prove hosted audit execution, or establish
[codex] +vulnerability-review parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] diff --git a/.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml b/.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..76b5139b5b89ba9eaa42e10855b5669d75774973
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Audit
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '17 6 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +concurrency:
[codex] +  group: dependency-audit
[codex] +  cancel-in-progress: false
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  npm-audit:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up Node.js
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          node-version: '22'
[codex] +          cache: 'npm'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Prepare report directory
[codex] +        run: mkdir -p dependency-audit-reports
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install locked dependencies
[codex] +        run: npm ci
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Capture full and production audits
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -u
[codex] +
[codex] +          set +e
[codex] +          npm audit --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.stderr.log
[codex] +          full_status=$?
[codex] +          npm audit --omit=dev --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.stderr.log
[codex] +          production_status=$?
[codex] +          set -e
[codex] +
[codex] +          audit_operational_failure=0
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit() {
[codex] +            scope="$1"
[codex] +            report="$2"
[codex] +            command_status="$3"
[codex] +
[codex] +            if [[ ! -s "${report}" ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced no JSON report." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e 'type == "object"' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced invalid JSON." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if jq -e '.error != null' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              jq -r '"npm audit error: \(.error.code // "unknown"): \(.error.summary // .error.message // "no message")"' "${report}" >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total | numbers' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit JSON has no vulnerability metadata." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            total="$(jq -r '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total' "${report}")"
[codex] +            if [[ "${command_status}" -eq 0 && "${total}" -ne 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -eq 1 && "${total}" -eq 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -ne 0 && "${command_status}" -ne 1 ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit returned unexpected status ${command_status} for ${total} findings." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            jq -r --arg scope "${scope}" '
[codex] +              .metadata.vulnerabilities |
[codex] +              "| \($scope) | \(.critical) | \(.high) | \(.moderate) | \(.low) | \(.total) |"
[codex] +            ' "${report}" >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          }
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            echo '## npm audit results'
[codex] +            echo
[codex] +            echo '| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total |'
[codex] +            echo '| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |'
[codex] +          } >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Full dependency tree" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            "${full_status}"
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Production dependencies" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            "${production_status}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            printf 'full_status=%s\n' "${full_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'production_status=%s\n' "${production_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'operational_failure=%s\n' "${audit_operational_failure}"
[codex] +          } > dependency-audit-reports/status.txt
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${audit_operational_failure}" -ne 0 ]]; then
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${full_status}" -eq 1 || "${production_status}" -eq 1 ]]; then
[codex] +            echo 'Vulnerability findings were retained as evidence and do not fail this scheduled report.' >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload audit reports
[codex] +        if: ${{ always() }}
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: npm-audit-reports
[codex] +          path: dependency-audit-reports/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: warn
[codex] +          retention-days: 7

[codex] +Missing or malformed JSON, an npm-reported registry error, an unexpected command
[codex] +status, or an `npm ci` failure does fail the workflow. Installation errors remain
[codex] +separate in the named install step's log, while audit errors and partial report
[codex] +files remain available through the always-upload artifact step.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free onboarding run on 2026-09-06 reproduced the saved review
[codex] +against the unchanged lockfile:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total | Top-level vulnerable paths |
[codex] +| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] +| Full dependency tree | 3 | 13 | 2 | 2 | 20 | `@babel/core`, `@humanfs/node`, `@vitest/coverage-v8`, `@vitest/ui`, `axios`, `brace-expansion`, `browserslist`, `fflate`, `flatted`, `form-data`, `js-yaml`, `nanoid`, `picomatch`, `postcss`, `postcss-selector-parser`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom`, `vite`, `vitest`, `ws` |
[codex] +| Production dependencies | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 4 | `axios`, `form-data`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom` |
[codex] +
[codex] +Both commands returned status 1 because findings exist, produced valid
[codex] +vulnerability metadata, and reported no registry error. Compare advisory and
[codex] +package identities in future runs rather than relying on the historical total:
[codex] +new advisories can appear while old ones are remediated.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachability and runtime applicability remain human triage. In particular,
[codex] +development-server findings are not production-browser findings, and this SPA
[codex] +does not use React Server Components or React Router's server/framework modes.
[codex] +Those distinctions inform priority but do not justify dropping findings from the
[codex] +full report.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. The existing `Build`
[codex] +pull-request workflow runs locked installation, ESLint, the Vitest coverage gate,
[codex] +the production-smoke CSP gate, and the standard TypeScript/Vite bundle. Reviewers
[codex] +must also inspect release notes, the resolved lockfile change, browser and SSR/RSC
[codex] +applicability, strict-CSP behavior, and Node/image platform compatibility.
[codex] +
[codex] +The [development guide](development.md#repository-commands) owns local build and
[codex] +validation commands. CSP-sensitive dependency changes must also follow the
[codex] +[architecture CSP contract](architecture.md#content-security-policy) and the
[codex] +[external browser harness](testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness); the bot
[codex] +cannot infer runtime stylesheet injection or mobile/browser compatibility from
[codex] +a successful package lookup.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +No GitHub, registry, or Mend credentials are required or used during local
[codex] +preparation. The activation operator must complete and retain these checks:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration preset, install the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, and retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving the preset resolves and npm, lockfile-maintenance,
[codex] +   Dockerfile, GitHub Actions, and setup-node inputs are discovered without
[codex] +   authentication, configuration, timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. Dispatch or observe `.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` on trusted
[codex] +   `main`. Retain the successful workflow URL and `npm-audit-reports` artifact
[codex] +   proving `npm ci` succeeded and both raw JSON scopes were captured. A findings
[codex] +   exit status is expected; installation, registry, and malformed-report errors
[codex] +   must still fail the job.
[codex] +3. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   Dependabot update pull requests remain disabled. Retain alert URLs and map
[codex] +   advisory/package identities to the two audit reports, including explained
[codex] +   misses and production-versus-development applicability.
[codex] +4. Retain the successful existing `Build` workflow URL for representative bot
[codex] +   pull requests, including lockfile maintenance and a toolchain-major proposal
[codex] +   after dashboard approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns final cross-repository acceptance. Local
[codex] +preparation does not activate Renovate, prove hosted audit execution, or establish
[codex] +vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Audit
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '17 6 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +concurrency:
[codex] +  group: dependency-audit
[codex] +  cancel-in-progress: false
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  npm-audit:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up Node.js
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          node-version: '22'
[codex] +          cache: 'npm'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Prepare report directory
[codex] +        run: mkdir -p dependency-audit-reports
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install locked dependencies
[codex] +        run: npm ci
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Capture full and production audits
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -u
[codex] +
[codex] +          set +e
[codex] +          npm audit --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.stderr.log
[codex] +          full_status=$?
[codex] +          npm audit --omit=dev --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.stderr.log
[codex] +          production_status=$?
[codex] +          set -e
[codex] +
[codex] +          audit_operational_failure=0
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit() {
[codex] +            scope="$1"
[codex] +            report="$2"
[codex] +            command_status="$3"
[codex] +
[codex] +            if [[ ! -s "${report}" ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced no JSON report." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e 'type == "object"' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced invalid JSON." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if jq -e '.error != null' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              jq -r '"npm audit error: \(.error.code // "unknown"): \(.error.summary // .error.message // "no message")"' "${report}" >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total | numbers' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit JSON has no vulnerability metadata." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            total="$(jq -r '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total' "${report}")"
[codex] +            if [[ "${command_status}" -eq 0 && "${total}" -ne 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -eq 1 && "${total}" -eq 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -ne 0 && "${command_status}" -ne 1 ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit returned unexpected status ${command_status} for ${total} findings." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            jq -r --arg scope "${scope}" '
[codex] +              .metadata.vulnerabilities |
[codex] +              "| \($scope) | \(.critical) | \(.high) | \(.moderate) | \(.low) | \(.total) |"
[codex] +            ' "${report}" >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          }
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            echo '## npm audit results'
[codex] +            echo
[codex] +            echo '| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total |'
[codex] +            echo '| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |'
[codex] +          } >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Full dependency tree" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            "${full_status}"
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Production dependencies" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            "${production_status}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            printf 'full_status=%s\n' "${full_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'production_status=%s\n' "${production_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'operational_failure=%s\n' "${audit_operational_failure}"
[codex] +          } > dependency-audit-reports/status.txt
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${audit_operational_failure}" -ne 0 ]]; then
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${full_status}" -eq 1 || "${production_status}" -eq 1 ]]; then
[codex] +            echo 'Vulnerability findings were retained as evidence and do not fail this scheduled report.' >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload audit reports
[codex] +        if: ${{ always() }}
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: npm-audit-reports
[codex] +          path: dependency-audit-reports/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: warn
[codex] +          retention-days: 7

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration and audit workflow prepared; automation is not
[codex] +installed or active. Authenticated acceptance is pending the system-wide
[codex] +activation phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +This repository uses Renovate as the sole owner of dependency-update pull
[codex] +requests. GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts provide alert-only
[codex] +vulnerability evidence; Dependabot version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +requests must remain disabled. The shared operating policy is owned by the
[codex] +[orchestration dependency-automation guide](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the Budget Analyzer shared preset. Renovate discovers:
[codex] +
[codex] +- direct npm dependencies and lockfile selections from `package.json` and
[codex] +  `package-lock.json`;
[codex] +- Node and NGINX build/runtime images, tags, and digests in all three
[codex] +  Dockerfiles;
[codex] +- pinned GitHub Actions and `actions/setup-node` runtime inputs in the workflow
[codex] +  files.
[codex] +
[codex] +Routine updates and lockfile maintenance run on the shared weekly schedule.
[codex] +React and React DOM updates are grouped, as are the Vitest runner, UI, and
[codex] +coverage packages. The shared policy disables automerge, keeps patch, minor,
[codex] +and major proposals distinguishable, and requires Dependency Dashboard approval
[codex] +for majors. Toolchain majors such as TypeScript, Vite, Vitest, ESLint, Tailwind,
[codex] +React Router, and Node therefore remain visible for compatibility review rather
[codex] +than being excluded or constrained to historical target versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Lockfile maintenance is discovery work, not permission to run `npm audit fix` or
[codex] +silently refresh the checked-in lockfile during onboarding. Review dependency
[codex] +and lockfile diffs together. For image updates, retain immutable digests and
[codex] +verify the selected tag/digest supports the required platform before merging.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Audit evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` runs weekly and on manual dispatch. It
[codex] +installs exactly `package-lock.json` with `npm ci`, then independently captures:
[codex] +
[codex] +- `npm audit --json` as `npm-audit-full.json`;
[codex] +- `npm audit --omit=dev --json` as `npm-audit-production.json`.
[codex] +
[codex] +Download the `npm-audit-reports` artifact from the workflow run to compare the
[codex] +raw JSON reports. The artifact is retained for seven days and also includes each
[codex] +command's stderr and `status.txt`. The job summary presents severity counts for
[codex] +the two scopes.
[codex] +
[codex] +An npm audit exit status caused by findings does not fail this scheduled report;
[codex] +the existing backlog remains visible without blocking unrelated pull requests.
[codex] +Missing or malformed JSON, an npm-reported registry error, an unexpected command
[codex] +status, or an `npm ci` failure does fail the workflow. Installation errors remain
[codex] +separate in the named install step's log, while audit errors and partial report
[codex] +files remain available through the always-upload artifact step.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free onboarding run on 2026-09-06 reproduced the saved review
[codex] +against the unchanged lockfile:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total | Top-level vulnerable paths |
[codex] +| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] +| Full dependency tree | 3 | 13 | 2 | 2 | 20 | `@babel/core`, `@humanfs/node`, `@vitest/coverage-v8`, `@vitest/ui`, `axios`, `brace-expansion`, `browserslist`, `fflate`, `flatted`, `form-data`, `js-yaml`, `nanoid`, `picomatch`, `postcss`, `postcss-selector-parser`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom`, `vite`, `vitest`, `ws` |
[codex] +| Production dependencies | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 4 | `axios`, `form-data`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom` |
[codex] +
[codex] +Both commands returned status 1 because findings exist, produced valid
[codex] +vulnerability metadata, and reported no registry error. Compare advisory and
[codex] +package identities in future runs rather than relying on the historical total:
[codex] +new advisories can appear while old ones are remediated.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachability and runtime applicability remain human triage. In particular,
[codex] +development-server findings are not production-browser findings, and this SPA
[codex] +does not use React Server Components or React Router's server/framework modes.
[codex] +Those distinctions inform priority but do not justify dropping findings from the
[codex] +full report.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. The existing `Build`
[codex] +pull-request workflow runs locked installation, ESLint, the Vitest coverage gate,
[codex] +the production-smoke CSP gate, and the standard TypeScript/Vite bundle. Reviewers
[codex] +must also inspect release notes, the resolved lockfile change, browser and SSR/RSC
[codex] +applicability, strict-CSP behavior, and Node/image platform compatibility.
[codex] +
[codex] +The [development guide](development.md#repository-commands) owns local build and
[codex] +validation commands. CSP-sensitive dependency changes must also follow the
[codex] +[architecture CSP contract](architecture.md#content-security-policy) and the
[codex] +[external browser harness](testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness); the bot
[codex] +cannot infer runtime stylesheet injection or mobile/browser compatibility from
[codex] +a successful package lookup.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +

[codex] +

[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving the preset resolves and npm, lockfile-maintenance,

[codex] +   exit status is expected; installation, registry, and malformed-report errors
[codex] +   must still fail the job.

[codex] +   pull requests, including lockfile maintenance and a toolchain-major proposal
[codex] +   after dashboard approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns final cross-repository acceptance. Local

[codex] +name: Dependency Audit
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '17 6 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +concurrency:
[codex] +  group: dependency-audit
[codex] +  cancel-in-progress: false
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  npm-audit:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up Node.js
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          node-version: '22'
[codex] +          cache: 'npm'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Prepare report directory
[codex] +        run: mkdir -p dependency-audit-reports
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install locked dependencies
[codex] +        run: npm ci
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Capture full and production audits
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -u
[codex] +
[codex] +          set +e
[codex] +          npm audit --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.stderr.log
[codex] +          full_status=$?
[codex] +          npm audit --omit=dev --json \
[codex] +            > dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            2> dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.stderr.log
[codex] +          production_status=$?
[codex] +          set -e
[codex] +
[codex] +          audit_operational_failure=0
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit() {
[codex] +            scope="$1"
[codex] +            report="$2"
[codex] +            command_status="$3"
[codex] +
[codex] +            if [[ ! -s "${report}" ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced no JSON report." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e 'type == "object"' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit produced invalid JSON." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if jq -e '.error != null' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              jq -r '"npm audit error: \(.error.code // "unknown"): \(.error.summary // .error.message // "no message")"' "${report}" >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            if ! jq -e '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total | numbers' "${report}" > /dev/null; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit JSON has no vulnerability metadata." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            total="$(jq -r '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total' "${report}")"
[codex] +            if [[ "${command_status}" -eq 0 && "${total}" -ne 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -eq 1 && "${total}" -eq 0 ]] \
[codex] +              || [[ "${command_status}" -ne 0 && "${command_status}" -ne 1 ]]; then
[codex] +              echo "${scope} npm audit returned unexpected status ${command_status} for ${total} findings." >&2
[codex] +              audit_operational_failure=1
[codex] +              return
[codex] +            fi
[codex] +
[codex] +            jq -r --arg scope "${scope}" '
[codex] +              .metadata.vulnerabilities |
[codex] +              "| \($scope) | \(.critical) | \(.high) | \(.moderate) | \(.low) | \(.total) |"
[codex] +            ' "${report}" >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          }
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            echo '## npm audit results'
[codex] +            echo
[codex] +            echo '| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total |'
[codex] +            echo '| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |'
[codex] +          } >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Full dependency tree" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-full.json \
[codex] +            "${full_status}"
[codex] +          classify_audit \
[codex] +            "Production dependencies" \
[codex] +            dependency-audit-reports/npm-audit-production.json \
[codex] +            "${production_status}"
[codex] +
[codex] +          {
[codex] +            printf 'full_status=%s\n' "${full_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'production_status=%s\n' "${production_status}"
[codex] +            printf 'operational_failure=%s\n' "${audit_operational_failure}"
[codex] +          } > dependency-audit-reports/status.txt
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${audit_operational_failure}" -ne 0 ]]; then
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${full_status}" -eq 1 || "${production_status}" -eq 1 ]]; then
[codex] +            echo 'Vulnerability findings were retained as evidence and do not fail this scheduled report.' >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload audit reports
[codex] +        if: ${{ always() }}
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: npm-audit-reports
[codex] +          path: dependency-audit-reports/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: warn
[codex] +          retention-days: 7

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,116 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration and audit workflow prepared; automation is not
[codex] +installed or active. Authenticated acceptance is pending the system-wide
[codex] +activation phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +This repository uses Renovate as the sole owner of dependency-update pull
[codex] +requests. GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts provide alert-only
[codex] +vulnerability evidence; Dependabot version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +requests must remain disabled. The shared operating policy is owned by the
[codex] +[orchestration dependency-automation guide](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the Budget Analyzer shared preset. Renovate discovers:
[codex] +
[codex] +- direct npm dependencies and lockfile selections from `package.json` and
[codex] +  `package-lock.json`;
[codex] +- Node and NGINX build/runtime images, tags, and digests in all three
[codex] +  Dockerfiles;
[codex] +- pinned GitHub Actions and `actions/setup-node` runtime inputs in the workflow
[codex] +  files.
[codex] +
[codex] +Routine updates and lockfile maintenance run on the shared weekly schedule.
[codex] +React and React DOM updates are grouped, as are the Vitest runner, UI, and
[codex] +coverage packages. The shared policy disables automerge, keeps patch, minor,
[codex] +and major proposals distinguishable, and requires Dependency Dashboard approval
[codex] +for majors. Toolchain majors such as TypeScript, Vite, Vitest, ESLint, Tailwind,
[codex] +React Router, and Node therefore remain visible for compatibility review rather
[codex] +than being excluded or constrained to historical target versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Lockfile maintenance is discovery work, not permission to run `npm audit fix` or
[codex] +silently refresh the checked-in lockfile during onboarding. Review dependency
[codex] +and lockfile diffs together. For image updates, retain immutable digests and
[codex] +verify the selected tag/digest supports the required platform before merging.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Audit evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` runs weekly and on manual dispatch. It
[codex] +installs exactly `package-lock.json` with `npm ci`, then independently captures:
[codex] +
[codex] +- `npm audit --json` as `npm-audit-full.json`;
[codex] +- `npm audit --omit=dev --json` as `npm-audit-production.json`.
[codex] +
[codex] +Download the `npm-audit-reports` artifact from the workflow run to compare the
[codex] +raw JSON reports. The artifact is retained for seven days and also includes each
[codex] +command's stderr and `status.txt`. The job summary presents severity counts for
[codex] +the two scopes.
[codex] +
[codex] +An npm audit exit status caused by findings does not fail this scheduled report;
[codex] +the existing backlog remains visible without blocking unrelated pull requests.
[codex] +Missing or malformed JSON, an npm-reported registry error, an unexpected command
[codex] +status, or an `npm ci` failure does fail the workflow. Installation errors remain
[codex] +separate in the named install step's log, while audit errors and partial report
[codex] +files remain available through the always-upload artifact step.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free onboarding run on 2026-09-06 reproduced the saved review
[codex] +against the unchanged lockfile:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Scope | Critical | High | Moderate | Low | Total | Top-level vulnerable paths |
[codex] +| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] +| Full dependency tree | 3 | 13 | 2 | 2 | 20 | `@babel/core`, `@humanfs/node`, `@vitest/coverage-v8`, `@vitest/ui`, `axios`, `brace-expansion`, `browserslist`, `fflate`, `flatted`, `form-data`, `js-yaml`, `nanoid`, `picomatch`, `postcss`, `postcss-selector-parser`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom`, `vite`, `vitest`, `ws` |
[codex] +| Production dependencies | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 4 | `axios`, `form-data`, `react-router`, `react-router-dom` |
[codex] +
[codex] +Both commands returned status 1 because findings exist, produced valid
[codex] +vulnerability metadata, and reported no registry error. Compare advisory and
[codex] +package identities in future runs rather than relying on the historical total:
[codex] +new advisories can appear while old ones are remediated.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachability and runtime applicability remain human triage. In particular,
[codex] +development-server findings are not production-browser findings, and this SPA
[codex] +does not use React Server Components or React Router's server/framework modes.
[codex] +Those distinctions inform priority but do not justify dropping findings from the
[codex] +full report.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. The existing `Build`
[codex] +pull-request workflow runs locked installation, ESLint, the Vitest coverage gate,
[codex] +the production-smoke CSP gate, and the standard TypeScript/Vite bundle. Reviewers
[codex] +must also inspect release notes, the resolved lockfile change, browser and SSR/RSC
[codex] +applicability, strict-CSP behavior, and Node/image platform compatibility.
[codex] +
[codex] +The [development guide](development.md#repository-commands) owns local build and
[codex] +validation commands. CSP-sensitive dependency changes must also follow the
[codex] +[architecture CSP contract](architecture.md#content-security-policy) and the
[codex] +[external browser harness](testing-guide.md#external-browser-harness); the bot
[codex] +cannot infer runtime stylesheet injection or mobile/browser compatibility from
[codex] +a successful package lookup.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +No GitHub, registry, or Mend credentials are required or used during local
[codex] +preparation. The activation operator must complete and retain these checks:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration preset, install the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, and retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving the preset resolves and npm, lockfile-maintenance,
[codex] +   Dockerfile, GitHub Actions, and setup-node inputs are discovered without
[codex] +   authentication, configuration, timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. Dispatch or observe `.github/workflows/dependency-audit.yml` on trusted
[codex] +   `main`. Retain the successful workflow URL and `npm-audit-reports` artifact
[codex] +   proving `npm ci` succeeded and both raw JSON scopes were captured. A findings
[codex] +   exit status is expected; installation, registry, and malformed-report errors
[codex] +   must still fail the job.
[codex] +3. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   Dependabot update pull requests remain disabled. Retain alert URLs and map
[codex] +   advisory/package identities to the two audit reports, including explained
[codex] +   misses and production-versus-development applicability.
[codex] +4. Retain the successful existing `Build` workflow URL for representative bot
[codex] +   pull requests, including lockfile maintenance and a toolchain-major proposal
[codex] +   after dashboard approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns final cross-repository acceptance. Local
[codex] +preparation does not activate Renovate, prove hosted audit execution, or establish
[codex] +vulnerability-review parity.
